      "calendar": "Yahoo official 2026-27 important game dates (https://basketball.fantasysports.yahoo.com/nba/gamedates); opponents from the 12 League Members screenshots",
      "fixtures": "Known fixtures plus an explicit, seeded representation of the unresolved NBA Cup / TBD team-games (source feed drops them at 80/team); no specific matchups invented. NBA teams fixed to the projection snapshot",
      "weeks": "20 league weeks simulated (17 regular + 3 playoff). W1 is 6 days; W7 and W17 are 14-day periods with only 6 and 8 NBA game-days, so period length is not a games-played proxy.",
      "injuries": "Synthetic Markov episodes calibrated approximately to GP/82; injury share is 75% scaled down for low-MPG role players, remainder isolated rest/role absences. No actual injury diagnosis inferred.",
      "il": "Three IL slots; medium/long episodes eligible after configurable delay. Returning players require an active-slot opening.",
      "waivers": "Daily pregame processing; dropped players wait configured calendar days; unsuccessful bids consume no acquisition or budget. Initial waiver order is seeded random, winning bidders move to end for continual rolling tiebreak. Starting FAB/minimum bid are assumptions; old ending balances give only weak spending priors.",
      "tiebreaks": "Category win percentage, then head-to-head category percentage, then last-period category percentage, then common seeded lottery for regular-season ties. Playoff ties use regular head-to-head category percentage, then higher seed. Verify exact Yahoo fallbacks.",
      "streaming": "Shared free-agent pool, four adds per scoring period by default (extended 14-day periods keep four unless the optional double cap is enabled), three IL slots, no future-health or box-score oracle. Source Moves totals used only as weak ordinal regular-season activity priors.",
      "eligibility": "placement (interpretation of eliminated-team locking is unverified)",
      "performance": "Coherent sampled shooting counts plus shared minute/weekly-form variability; projected points reconstructed from shooting makes due rounding. Not calibrated player covariance.",
      "limits": "Population restricted to imported projections; missing Yahoo-eligible players cannot be invented. Fixed NBA teams, no fantasy/NBA trades or schedule revisions, no historical role/injury calibration or injury-driven teammate promotions, no speculative injured-FA stash purchases. No calibration of championship frequencies."
    }

## Design
1. Screen the current eligible pool using unpruned production recommendations, supplied ADP, neutral category value, and keeper-aware natural first-five draws. Elite fallers are separate contingencies.
2. Discover #43/#54 pools from production top-15, category-neutral value, market value, and stocks/shooting/big/creation directions on varied legal paths. Vary #43 before observing #54.
3. Compile deterministic availability-only policies from ADP-primary discovery. Compare simple, conditional, wait, direction and full-board production policies. No averaging models to select a winner.
4. Use paired draft randomness and shared season seeds. Each source index has distinct draft and season seeds. Conditional first-pick comparisons use only overlapping available-first-pick paths. Unavailable candidates are skipped, never protected from opponents.
5. Retain the three requested branches plus strategically distinct exploratory alternatives. Freeze chosen rules before confirmation. Confirm on a disjoint seed phase including new drafts, with a simple policy and full-board comparator where time permits.
6. Stress tests replace two opponent selections on an otherwise legal partial board. Displaced players return to the pool. These deliberately constructed cases are never counted as natural availability.

## Season management / objective
Championship is primary; qualification and advancement are diagnostics. Oscar uses aggressive streaming; opponents use the same fixed observed-activity assumptions for every compared draft policy. Daily lineups use projection-based position-constrained selection and the production continuation build; no future health/box-score access. Four acquisitions/period, three IL slots, placement eligibility, default FAB/waivers. Final diagnostic category comparisons use recorded completed rosters with no imposed punt; temporary last evaluated build is retained separately.
{
  "calendar": "Yahoo official 2026-27 important game dates (https://basketball.fantasysports.yahoo.com/nba/gamedates); opponents from the 12 League Members screenshots",
  "fixtures": "Known fixtures plus an explicit, seeded representation of the unresolved NBA Cup / TBD team-games (source feed drops them at 80/team); no specific matchups invented. NBA teams fixed to the projection snapshot",
  "weeks": "20 league weeks simulated (17 regular + 3 playoff). W1 is 6 days; W7 and W17 are 14-day periods with only 6 and 8 NBA game-days, so period length is not a games-played proxy.",
  "injuries": "Synthetic Markov episodes calibrated approximately to GP/82; injury share is 75% scaled down for low-MPG role players, remainder isolated rest/role absences. No actual injury diagnosis inferred.",
  "il": "Three IL slots; medium/long episodes eligible after configurable delay. Returning players require an active-slot opening.",
  "waivers": "Daily pregame processing; dropped players wait configured calendar days; unsuccessful bids consume no acquisition or budget. Initial waiver order is seeded random, winning bidders move to end for continual rolling tiebreak. Starting FAB/minimum bid are assumptions; old ending balances give only weak spending priors.",
  "tiebreaks": "Category win percentage, then head-to-head category percentage, then last-period category percentage, then common seeded lottery for regular-season ties. Playoff ties use regular head-to-head category percentage, then higher seed. Verify exact Yahoo fallbacks.",
  "streaming": "Shared free-agent pool, four adds per scoring period by default (extended 14-day periods keep four unless the optional double cap is enabled), three IL slots, no future-health or box-score oracle. Source Moves totals used only as weak ordinal regular-season activity priors.",
  "eligibility": "placement (interpretation of eliminated-team locking is unverified)",
  "performance": "Coherent sampled shooting counts plus shared minute/weekly-form variability; projected points reconstructed from shooting makes due rounding. Not calibrated player covariance.",
  "limits": "Population restricted to imported projections; missing Yahoo-eligible players cannot be invented. Fixed NBA teams, no fantasy/NBA trades or schedule revisions, no historical role/injury calibration or injury-driven teammate promotions, no speculative injured-FA stash purchases. No calibration of championship frequencies."
}

## Uncertainty / multiplicity
Independent source-draft path is the cluster. Worlds paired across policies, distinct across paths. Bootstrap intervals pointwise; bounded simultaneous intervals protect reported comparison family. ±5 percentage-point equivalence requires entire simultaneous interval inside margin. No calibrated odds. Selection on screening/deep data is biased; confirmation is separate.
The bounded simultaneous interval uses Hoeffding on per-path paired differences in [-1,1], with a Bonferroni family size. It is deliberately conservative, finite-sample, and nondegenerate when no championships occur. Bootstrap intervals are descriptive pointwise intervals and can be unstable with few paths. Neither quantifies model misspecification. Availability-conditioning limits cross-first-pick comparability.

## Limitations
- Opponent models share a fitted pooled baseline; agreement is not independent validation.
- Same-round keeper-cost rule remains unverified; source ownership reconciled.
- Hashtag positions are provisional Yahoo eligibility; injury/role projections are a fixed snapshot.
- Availability is conditional on modeled opponents and keepers, not calibrated live probabilities.
- Small conditional subgroups and boards outside support require the lower-certainty fallback.
- The full-board production policy is a diagnostic comparator, not draft-day simulation advice.
